    '/** Scarica in background, uno alla volta e a browser inattivo, i chunk delle pagine e dei tab della\r\n' +
    " * cartella. Idempotente: il browser tiene i moduli gia' importati, quindi la navigazione successiva\r\n" +
    ' * non attende la rete. */\r\n' +
    'function preloadRouteModules(): void {\r\n' +
    '  if (routeModulesPreloaded) return;\r\n' +
    '  routeModulesPreloaded = true;\r\n' +
    '  // Prima le pagine di atterraggio (dashboard, lista, scheda) in parallelo, poi il resto una\r\n' +
    '  // alla volta a browser inattivo.\r\n' +
    '  const first: Array<() => Promise<unknown>> = [\r\n' +
    '    routeLoaders.OperatorDashboard,\r\n' +
    '    routeLoaders.AdminDashboard,\r\n' +
    '    routeLoaders.PatientList,\r\n' +
    '    routeLoaders.PatientDetail,\r\n' +
    '  ];\r\n' +
    '  const rest: Array<() => Promise<unknown>> = [\r\n' +
    '    ...Object.values(routeLoaders).filter((loader) => !first.includes(loader)),\r\n' +
    '    () =>\r\n' +
    "      import('./components/operator/PatientDetailLazyTabs').then((m) =>\r\n" +
    '        m.preloadPatientDetailTabs(),\r\n' +
    '      ),\r\n' +
    '  ];\r\n' +
    '  const idle = (fn: () => void) =>\r\n' +
    "    typeof window.requestIdleCallback === 'function'\r\n" +
    '      ? window.requestIdleCallback(() => fn(), { timeout: 2000 })\r\n' +
    '      : window.setTimeout(fn, 200);\r\n' +
    '  const next = () => {\r\n' +
    '    const loader = rest.shift();\r\n' +
    '    if (!loader) return;\r\n' +
    '    loader()\r\n' +
    '      .catch(() => undefined)\r\n' +
    '      .finally(() => idle(next));\r\n' +
    '  };\r\n' +
    '  void Promise.allSettled(first.map((loader) => loader())).finally(() => idle(next));\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'function PageLoading() {\r\n' +
    '  return (\r\n' +
    '    <div className="page-loading" role="status" aria-live="polite">\r\n' +
    '      <span className="page-loading__spinner" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n' +
    '      Caricamento modulo…\r\n' +
    '    </div>\r\n' +
    '  );\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'class LazyLoadBoundary extends Component<{ children: ReactNode }, { failed: boolean }> {\r\n' +
    '  state = { failed: false };\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  static getDerivedStateFromError() {\r\n' +
    '    return { failed: true };\r\n' +
    '  }\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  render() {\r\n' +
    '    if (this.state.failed) {\r\n' +
    '      return (\r\n' +
    '        <div className="page-load-error" role="alert">\r\n' +
    '          <strong>Il modulo non è stato caricato.</strong>\r\n' +
    '          <span>Potrebbe essere disponibile una versione più recente dell’applicazione.</span>\r\n' +
    '          <button type="button" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>\r\n' +
    '            Ricarica ClinicOS\r\n' +
    '          </button>\r\n' +
    '        </div>\r\n' +
    '      );\r\n' +
    '    }\r\n' +
    '    return this.props.children;\r\n' +
    '  }\r\n' +
